 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1125</td> <td>Licences for computer software</td> <td>Licences acquired separately from computer software shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1129</td> <td>Other rights to intangible investments</td> <td>Other previously non-classified rights of intangible investments - trade marks, service marks, geographical indications which are used to distinguish some goods and services from goods and services of other producers or traders, designs, and other similar rights - shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1130</td> <td></td> <td>Other intangible investments</td> <td>Previously non-classified intangible investments which correspond to the definition of the asset, i.e., the value of the asset can be reliably evaluated and there is a possibility to receive economic benefits in the future or to use it for ensuring of the functions (for example, dedicated knowledge, franchises) shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1131</td> <td>Intangible cultural and historical monuments and other objects</td> <td>Original films, sound recordings, and other cultural and historical monuments and other objects which correspond to the definition of the asset shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1139</td> <td>Other previously non-classified intangible investments</td> <td>Other previously non-classified intangible investments shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1140</td> <td></td> <td>Construction of intangible investments</td> <td>Costs which are directly attributable to the construction and preparation of intangible investments for the intended use until putting of the intangible investment in use shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1141</td> <td>Construction of intangible cultural and historical monuments and other objects</td> <td>Costs which are directly attributable to the construction and preparation of intangible cultural and historical monuments and other objects for the intended use until their putting into use shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1149</td> <td>Construction of other previously non-classified intangible investments</td> <td>Costs which are directly attributable to the construction and preparation of other previously non-classified intangible investments for the intended use until their putting into use shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1160</td> <td></td> <td colspan="2">[6 September 2022]</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1180</td> <td></td> <td>Prepayments for intangible investments</td> <td>Prepayments (advance payments) made for the acquisition and construction of intangible investments and the impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1181</td> <td>Prepayments for development measures and software</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1182</td> <td>Prepayments for licences, concessions and patents, trade marks and similar rights</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1184</td> <td>Prepayments for intangible investments in research of mineral resources and other similar non-produced intangible investments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1185</td> <td>Prepayments for other intangible investments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1187</td> <td>Impairment of prepayments for intangible investments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1190</td> <td></td> <td>Amortisation and impairment of intangible investments</td> <td>Impairment and amortisation of intangible investments which have been calculated in accordance with the intended useful life thereof shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1191</td> <td>Accumulated amortisation of development measures and software</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1192</td> <td>Accumulated amortisation of licences, concessions and patents, trade marks and similar rights</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1193</td> <td>Accumulated amortisation of other intangible investments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1195</td> <td>Accumulated amortisation of computer software</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1196</td> <td>Accumulated amortisation of intangible assets for the research of mineral resources and other similar non-produced intangible assets</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1197</td> <td>Accumulated amortisation of licences for computer software</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1199</td> <td>Impairment of intangible investments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>1200</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Property, plant and equipment</td> <td>Tangible and non-monetary assets which are intended for ensuring the functions of the budget institution, for administrative purposes, for the provision of services, for the production (supply) of goods and which are planned to be used for more than a year from the day of acquisition shall be accounted in the group of accounts. The initial recognition cost of a property, plant and equipment unit shall be determined in accordance with the laws and regulations in the field of the classification of budget expenditures</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1210</td> <td></td> <td>Land and structures</td> <td>Land under structures, cultivated land, land to be used for recreation and entertainment, structures, including equipment, installations and devices that are an integral part of such structures that are in the ownership or possession or under jurisdiction of the state shall be accounted in the group of accounts. Plots of land and structures shall be accounted in this account if they are intended for ensuring the functions and provision of services of the budget institution and they are planned to be used for more than a year from the day of acquisition: 1) land shall be accounted in land units according to the allocated cadastral designation number of the land unit;<br> 2) structures which are cultural (architectural) monuments shall be accounted in the accounts of similar property, plant and equipment or investment properties according to the use thereof (for example, residential buildings, non-residential buildings, structures), separating them in the analytical accounting;<br> 3) constructions, equipment, installations and devices of structures which are an integral part thereof shall be accounted together with the relevant structure</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1211</td> <td>Residential buildings</td> <td>Buildings which have been classified as residential buildings in accordance with the laws and regulations in the field of classification of structures shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1212</td> <td>Non-residential buildings</td> <td>Buildings which have been classified as non-residential buildings in accordance with the laws and regulations in the field of classification of structures shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1213</td> <td>Transport structures</td> <td>Streets, motor roads, pavements, parking lots, airport runways, railway track constructions, bridges, overpasses, tunnels, structures and constructions of ports and quaysides, equipment of motor roads and streets (for example, traffic signs, signal poles, barriers, traffic control devices) and other similar objects shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1214</td> <td>Land beneath structures</td> <td>Land on which structures or their foundations that are in the ownership or possession or under jurisdiction of the State or local government are built shall be accounted in the account. Courtyards, garden territories, and driveways that are considered an integral part of the structure shall also be accounted herein</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1215</td> <td>Cultivated land</td> <td>Land which is used in agriculture (including land for plantations and orchards) shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1216</td> <td>Land to be used for recreation and entertainment</td> <td>Land beneath park territories and recreational areas shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1217</td> <td>Other land</td> <td>Land beneath inland public waters and maritime coastal waters and the land part of the maritime coast, and also other previously non-classified land (for example, quarries, land of the cemetery territory, forest land) shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1218</td> <td>Engineering structures</td> <td>Engineering structures which have been classified in accordance with the laws and regulations in the field of classification of structures, except for the transport structures listed in the account 1213, shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1219</td> <td>Other immovable property</td> <td>Previously non-classified immovable property which cannot be classified as buildings or engineering structures (for example, grasslands arranged in parks, courtyards) shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1220</td> <td></td> <td>Technological equipment and machinery</td> <td>Technological equipment, measuring equipment, regulating devices, laboratory (including training laboratory), and medical installations and machinery which are used for ensuring the functions or provision of services of the budget institution and which are intended for the performance of specific successive technological operations shall be accounted in the account: 1) vehicles and computer hardware shall not be accounted in the composition of the technological equipment and machinery;<br> 2) spare parts and auxiliaries of technological equipment and machinery, which have an independent significance in the economic process and which are considered property, plant and equipment, shall be included in the composition of property, plant and equipment as separate accounting objects of the property, plant and equipment. Other spare parts and auxiliaries shall be accounted in the composition of the inventories and derecognised in expenditures when they are actually consumed (used)</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1230</td> <td></td> <td>Other property, plant and equipment</td> <td>Vehicles, fixed household assets, computer hardware, communication and office equipment, library funds, works of art and objects of art, valuables, objects of culture and art, assets in the composition of museum collections (except for immovable property), military combat vehicles and equipment and other previously non-classified property, plant and equipment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1231</td> <td>Vehicles</td> <td>Vehicles for the carriage of goods or passengers, and also other vehicles intended for ensuring the functions of the institution (goods and road passenger automobiles, buses, trolleybuses, motorcycles, bicycles, trailers and semi-trailers, watercraft, railway propulsion equipment, rolling stock of the railway and tram system, aircraft and other vehicles) shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1233</td> <td>Library funds</td> <td>Books (including school textbooks) and other printed publications, microfilms, sound recordings, and other information media which form a library fund shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1235</td> <td>Precious stones, precious metals, and other valuables</td> <td>Precious metals and precious stones which are not used in production processes and other valuables, collections, and articles of jewellery shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1236</td> <td>Literary and art original works, objects of culture and art</td> <td>Manuscripts, paintings, sculptures, and other tangible and non-monetary works and objects of art which are recognised as works of art, and also antique objects shall be accounted in the account. Also such culture and art objects which have been included in the composition of a museum collection and are not accounted in other accounts of property, plant and equipment according to the application thereof, regardless of the value thereof, shall be accounted in this account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1238</td> <td>Computer hardware, communications equipment, and other office equipment</td> <td>Computers, servers, photocopiers, fax machines, telephones, telephone exchanges, communications equipment, and other office equipment and devices thereof, and also individual software programs and computer programs which cannot be used separately and without which computers and servers are not able to operate shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1239</td> <td>Other previously non-classified property, plant and equipment</td> <td>Property, plant and equipment which are used for ensuring the economic activity of the budget institution and other previously non-classified property, plant and equipment, for example, video surveillance systems, access control systems, books, if they have not been accounted in the account 1233, shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1240</td> <td></td> <td>Construction of property, plant and equipment and unfinished building objects</td> <td>Costs which are attributable to the construction or building of a particular object until the day when object is put into use (service) shall be accounted in the group of accounts. Costs of installations and machinery which have been received but are not yet installed and put into use shall also be accounted in these accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1241</td> <td>Construction of property, plant and equipment</td> <td>Costs which are attributable to the construction of a particular property, plant and equipment until putting into use (service) thereof shall be accounted in the account. The value of the property, plant and equipment received but not installed or put into use, if the property, plant and equipment acquired are intended for transfer to the general government sector units, shall be accounted in the accounts of property, plant and equipment corresponding to theirs economic nature until the putting into use or transfer thereof to the general government sector units. Depreciation shall not be calculated until the property, plant and equipment is put into use</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1242</td> <td>Unfinished building objects</td> <td>Construction costs until putting into use (service) of the property, plant and equipment shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1250</td> <td></td> <td>State and local government properties transferred in the possession</td> <td>Long-term investments of the State and local governments which have been transferred for holding, except for lease, to capital companies, port authorities, or derived public entities shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1251</td> <td>State and local government land transferred for holding</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1252</td> <td>State and local government structures transferred for holding</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1254</td> <td>Unfinished building objects of the State and local governments transferred for holding</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1259</td> <td>Other properties of the State and local government transferred for holding</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1260</td> <td></td> <td>Biological and underground assets</td> <td>Underground assets, permanent plantations (orchards, plantings of trees and bushes, parks, forest stands), animals, and other similar assets to which renewal and changes of value while they grow are characteristic and which are not intended to be used in agricultural activity but for the purposes of scientific research, education, transport, entertainment or leisure, or safety and control shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1261</td> <td>Underground assets</td> <td>Explored inventories of mineral deposits which are located under the surface of ground and are economically usable, taking into account present technologies and the relevant prices, shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1262</td> <td>Orchards and other plantings of trees that regularly produce</td> <td>Trees that regularly produce and other plantings (including vine and bush) shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1263</td> <td>Forest stands</td> <td>Forest stands shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1264</td> <td>State and local government biological and underground assets transferred for holding</td> <td>Biological and underground assets of the State and local governments which have been transferred for holding, except for lease, to capital companies, port authorities, or derived public entities shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1269</td> <td>Other biological assets</td> <td>Animals, permanent plantings, and other similar assets the value of which changes while they grow and which are not considered underground assets, forest stands, orchards and other plantings that produce regularly shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1270</td> <td></td> <td>Long-term investments in leased property, plant and equipment</td> <td>Capital investments in structures or other property, plant and equipment (for example, reconstruction, restoration, renewal of buildings) which are not in possession of the State or local government, if such investments are permitted in rental, lease, or management contracts or are planned in investment projects, shall be accounted in the account. The relevant investments shall be accounted as improvements of the leased property</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1280</td> <td></td> <td>Prepayments for property, plant and equipment</td> <td>Prepayments (advance payments) made for the acquisition of property, plant and equipment and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1281</td> <td>Prepayments for land and structures</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1282</td> <td>Prepayments for technological equipment and machinery</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1283</td> <td>Prepayments for other property, plant and equipment</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1286</td> <td>Prepayments for biological and underground assets</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1287</td> <td>Impairment of prepayments for property, plant and equipment</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1289</td> <td>Other prepayments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1290</td> <td></td> <td>Accumulated depreciation and impairment of property, plant and equipment</td> <td>Depreciation and impairment of each group of property, plant and equipment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1291</td> <td>Accumulated depreciation of structures</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1292</td> <td>Accumulated depreciation of technological equipment and machinery</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1293</td> <td>Accumulated depreciation of other property, plant and equipment</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1295</td> <td>Accumulated depreciation of State and local government property transferred for holding</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1297</td> <td>Accumulated depreciation of long-term investments in the leased property, plant and equipment</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1298</td> <td>Impairment of biological and underground assets</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1299</td> <td>Impairment of property, plant and equipment</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>1300</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Long-term financial investments</td> <td>Financial investments which are intended to be held for more than one year from the balance sheet date and impairment of such investments shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1310</td> <td></td> <td>Participation in the capital of related capital companies</td> <td>Investments in capital companies in which the budget institution owns more than 50.001 per cent of the voting rights (subsidiary capital companies) and impairment of such investments shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1311</td> <td>Participation in the capital of related capital companies</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1319</td> <td>Impairment of participation in the capital of related capital companies</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1320</td> <td></td> <td>Participation in the capital of associated capital companies</td> <td>Investments in capital companies in which the budget institution owns from 20.001 to 50 per cent of the voting rights and impairment of such investments shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1321</td> <td>Participation in the capital of associated capital companies</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1329</td> <td>Impairment of participation in the capital of associated capital companies</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1330</td> <td></td> <td>Non-current loans</td> <td>Loans and accrued revenues the limit for the repayment of which exceeds one year from the balance sheet date, and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1331</td> <td>Non-current loans to related capital companies</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1332</td> <td>Non-current loans to associated capital companies</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1333</td> <td>Long-term accrued revenues from loans</td> <td>Accrued revenues from interest, liability, servicing, and other payments which have been earned until the end of the reporting period but the limit for the receipt of which is not later than a year after the balance sheet date and for which the corroborative payment document has not been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1338</td> <td>Impairment of non-current loans</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1339</td> <td>Other non-current loans</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1340</td> <td></td> <td>Long-term financial investments in securities</td> <td>Financial investments in securities the holding or maturity limit of which exceeds a year from the balance sheet date, accrued revenues, and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1343</td> <td>Long-term accrued revenues from financial investments in securities</td> <td>Accrued revenues from financial investments in securities which have been earned until the end of the reporting period but the limit for the receipt of which is not later than a year after the balance sheet date and for which the corroborative payment document has not been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1348</td> <td>Impairment of long-term financial investments in securities</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1349</td> <td>Long-term financial investments in securities</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1350</td> <td></td> <td>Other long-term financial investments</td> <td>Other financial investments the expected holding limit of which exceeds a year from the balance sheet date, accrued revenues, and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1351</td> <td>Long-term financial investments in international financial institutions</td> <td>Investment in international financial institutions (the World Bank (WB), the Council of Europe Development Bank (CEDB), the Multilateral Investment Guarantee Agency (MIGA), the European Bank for Reconstruction and Development (EBRD), and others, except for the International Monetary Fund (IMF)), if the budget institution has acquired such shares and intends to hold them for more than one year from the balance sheet date shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1354</td> <td>Non-current receivables for tier 2 funded pensions</td> <td>This account shall be used only the State Social Insurance Agency</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1355</td> <td>Long-term accrued revenues from other long-term financial investments</td> <td>Accrued revenues from other financial investments which have been earned until the end of the reporting period but the limit for the receipt of which is not later than a year after the balance sheet date and for which the corroborative payment document has not been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1358</td> <td>Impairment of other long-term financial investments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1359</td> <td>Other long-term financial investments</td> <td>Investments in the International Monetary Fund (IMF), if the State has acquired these shares and is intending to hold them for more than one year from the balance sheet date, investments in capital companies in which the budget institution owns less than 20 per cent of voting rights, if the State or local government has acquired these shares and is intending to hold them for more than a year from the balance sheet date, derived financial instruments, and other previously non-classified financial investments shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1360</td> <td></td> <td>Long-term deposits</td> <td>Long-term deposits the expected holding limit of which exceeds a year from the balance sheet date, accrued revenues, and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1363</td> <td>Long-term accrued revenues from long-term deposits</td> <td>Accrued revenues from deposits which have been earned until the end of the reporting period but the limit for the receipt of which is not later than a year after the balance sheet date and for which the corroborative payment document has not been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1368</td> <td>Impairment of long-term deposits</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1371</td> <td>Participation in related capital companies transferred for holding</td> <td>Investments in capital companies in which the budget institution owns more than 50.001 per cent of the voting rights and which the State or local government has intended to hold for more than one year from the balance sheet date, and which have been transferred for holding, except for lease, to capital companies, port authorities, or derived public entities shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1372</td> <td>Participation in associated capital companies transferred for holding</td> <td>Investments in capital companies in which the budget institution owns from 20.001 to 50 per cent of the voting rights and which the State or local government has intended to hold for more than one year from the balance sheet date, and which have been transferred for holding, except for lease, to capital companies, port authorities, or derived public entities shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1378</td> <td>Impairment of long-term financial investments transferred for holding</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1379</td> <td>Other long-term financial investments transferred for holding</td> <td>Investments in the capital of capital companies in which the budget institution owns less than 20 per cent of the voting rights and which the State or local government has intended to hold for more than one year from the balance sheet date, and which have been transferred for holding, except for lease, to capital companies, port authorities, or derived public entities shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1380</td> <td></td> <td>Prepayments for long-term financial investments</td> <td>Prepayments (advance payments) for the acquisition of long-term financial investments and impairment of prepayments shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>

 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1390</td> <td></td> <td>Non-current receivables for derived financial instruments</td> <td>Non-current receivables for derived financial instruments shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>1400</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Non-current receivables</td> <td>Receivables for which the specified limit for receipt is more than a year from the balance sheet date, prepaid expenditures, and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1410</td> <td></td> <td>Non-current receivables for projects (measures) financed from the European Union policy instruments and other foreign financial assistance</td> <td>Non-current receivables for projects (measures) financed from the European Union policy instruments and other foreign financial assistance, separating analytically receivables of each financing instrument or fund (for example, for projects of the Financial Instrument for Fisheries Guidance, projects of the European Agricultural Guidance and Guarantee Fund, projects of the European Social Fund, projects of the European Regional Development Fund) shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1420</td> <td></td> <td>Other non-current receivables</td> <td>Other previously non-classified non-current receivables, prepaid expenditures, and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>1421</td> <td>Non-current receivables for finance lease</td> <td>Receivables for finance lease (leasing) the limit for the repayment of which exceeds one year from the balance sheet date shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>

Integral parts of investment properties and movable properties which are an integral part of the lease contract shall be accounted and analytically separated in the composition of investment properties</td> </tr>

 <tr>

 <td></td> <td>1600</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Biological assets for agricultural activity</td> <td>Biological assets that are planned to be used in agricultural activity for more than one year, prepayments for biological assets, and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>1610</td> <td></td> <td>Biological assets for agricultural activity</td> <td>Forest stands the value of which changes while they grow and other assets which are planned to be used in agricultural activity for more than one year shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>

 <tr>
 <td>2000</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Current assets</td> <td>Assets which are used by the budget institution within the scope of the current operating cycle even if the cycle exceeds 12 months, including also assets which are mainly held for trade, and the current portion of non-current financial assets shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>2100</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Inventory</td> <td>Current assets intended for ensuring the operation of the budget institution or for disposal within the scope of operation of the budget institution shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2110</td> <td></td> <td>Raw materials and materials</td> <td>Raw materials and materials, materials that are intended for educational and scientific purposes, food products, medicinal products, medicine, medical materials, household materials, office stationery, heating fuel, fuel, lubricating oils, machinery equipment and spare parts (which are not property, plant and equipment), and also other previously non-classified raw materials and materials shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2111</td> <td>Materials for educational, scientific and other purposes</td> <td>Reagents and chemicals, laboratory utensils that are made of glass and other materials, metals (including precious metals), electro-technical materials, radio materials, photographic accessories, paper for the publication of study programmes, study means, and scientific works, experimental animals, books, and publications which are not accounted in the account 1233, food products for study needs and other materials for study needs and works of scientific research shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2112</td> <td>Food products</td> <td>In institutions that ensure catering services, food products shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2113</td> <td>Medicine, medicinal products and medical materials</td> <td>Medicine, medicinal products, medical materials and dressings, bacteriological, immuno-biological preparations, and other similar materials shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2114</td> <td>Household materials and office stationery</td> <td>Household materials for everyday needs (electric light bulbs, soap, brushes, washing powders, and other household materials), office supplies and office stationery, building materials and other inventories for a regular and capital renovation (for example, bricks, slates, cement, plumbing) shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2115</td> <td>Heating fuel, fuel, lubricants</td> <td>All types of heating fuel (firewood, coal, briquettes, peat), fuel, lubricants (petrol, motor oils), and other similar inventories shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2116</td> <td>Installations and machinery spare parts</td> <td>Spare parts intended for repair works and replacement of used parts (for example, for medical installations, tractors, harvesters, vehicles, military combat vehicles and equipment, computers) that are not property, plant and equipment shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2119</td> <td>Other materials</td> <td>Other previously non-classified raw materials and materials (for example, packaging, materials intended for the implementation of public relations and ensuring the administrative activity of the institution, ammunition and explosives) shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2120</td> <td></td> <td>Unfinished products and orders</td> <td>Costs accrued from such products, scientific and other orders and programmes which are under construction shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2130</td> <td></td> <td>Finished products, orders and inventories for disposal</td> <td>Produced products, goods and long-term investments that are intended for disposal (sale), and also finished orders shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2131</td> <td>Finished products</td> <td>Produced products (for example, products made in production (study) workshops, finished printed publications) the production process of which is completed shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2132</td> <td>Disposable inventories and long-term investments</td> <td>Goods and other inventories which have been acquired or are intended for disposal (sale, transfer) without further processing shall be accounted in the account. Non-current non-financial assets intended for disposal shall also be accounted in this account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2133</td> <td>Finished orders</td> <td>Finished scientific and other orders and programmes shall be accounted in the account according to contracts entered into</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2134</td> <td>Disposable State and local government inventories and long-term investments transferred for holding</td> <td>State and local government inventories and long-term investments that are in the ownership or possession of the budget institution, have been transferred for holding, except for lease, to State or local government capital companies, port authorities, or derived public entities, and are intended for disposal shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2140</td> <td></td> <td>Products of biological assets and inventories for their maintenance</td> <td>Products of biological assets, and also other inventories for their maintenance shall be included in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2141</td> <td>Products of biological assets</td> <td>Products of biological assets which have been obtained from biological assets, for example, wood, puppies, wool, apples, shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2142</td> <td>Animal fodder and seeds</td> <td>Seeds, hay, oats, and other types of animal fodder which are intended for the feeding and accommodation of animals and livestock shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2149</td> <td>Other inventories</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2150</td> <td></td> <td>State material reserves</td> <td>State material reserves (for example, grain, food reserves) in accordance with the laws and regulations regarding the formation of State material reserves shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2160</td> <td></td> <td>Equipment</td> <td>Equipment with long useful life, equipment with short useful life and other similar equipment, and also military equipment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2161</td> <td>Equipment with long useful life</td> <td>Equipment which is intended for ensuring the operation of the institution and does not differ from a property, plant and equipment by its functions and the acquisition or construction cost of one unit of the asset of which is not less than the initial recognition cost of one unit of the asset specified in the laws and regulations in the field of classification of budget expenditures, and which is not accounted in the account 2162 shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2162</td> <td>Clothing, footwear, linen and bedding</td> <td>Clothing, footwear, stage costumes, linen, bedding, and similar inventory shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2165</td> <td>State and local government equipment transferred for holding</td> <td>State and local government equipment that is in the ownership or possession of the budget institution and has been transferred for holding, except for lease, to capital companies, port authorities, or derived public entities shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2169</td> <td>Equipment with short useful life</td> <td>Equipment which is planned to be used for less than 12 months from the date of acquisition and which is not accounted in the account 2162 shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2170</td> <td></td> <td>Specialist military equipment and construction of specialist military equipment</td> <td>Special military equipment and the construction costs thereof which are necessary for ensuring the fulfilment of the military tasks of the National Armed Forces and the training related thereto and for the development of military skills, and which have arisen until 31 December 2018 shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2171</td> <td>Specialist military equipment</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2179</td> <td>Construction of specialist military equipment</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2180</td> <td></td> <td>Prepayments and impairment of prepayments for inventories</td> <td>Prepayments for the acquisition and formation of inventories and impairment of prepayments for inventories shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2181</td> <td>Prepayments for inventories</td> <td></td> </tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>2300</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Current receivables</td> <td>Receivables which have arisen until the end of the reporting period as a result of past transactions (events) (for example, receivables for the services provided, receivables for the assets disposed, tax receivables) and which are due for settlement within a year from the balance sheet date shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2310</td> <td></td> <td>Receivables from purchasers and commissioning parties</td> <td>Receivables from purchasers and commissioning parties which have arisen from selling assets, carrying out work or providing services and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2311</td> <td>Receivables from purchasers and commissioning parties</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2315</td> <td>Receivables for finance lease</td> <td>Current receivables for finance lease (leasing) with repayment limit within a year from the balance sheet date shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2316</td> <td>Receivables for operating lease</td> <td>Current receivables for operating lease services with repayment term within a year from the balance sheet date shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2317</td> <td>Receivables for interest and other payments</td> <td>Current receivables for interest and other payments (for example, finance lease, payments which have been deferred for more than 12 months, and other receivables from purchasers and commissioning parties) which have been earned until the end of the reporting period and for which the corroborative payment document has been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2318</td> <td>Impairment of receivables from purchasers and commissioning parties</td> <td>Impairment of receivables for finance lease, payments which have been deferred for more than 12 months, and other receivables from purchasers and commissioning parties shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2320</td> <td></td> <td>Receivables for projects (measures) financed from foreign financial assistance and the European Union policy instruments</td> <td>Receivables arisen during the implementation of the relevant projects, separating analytically the receivables of each financing instrument or fund (for example, for projects of the Financial Instrument for Fisheries Guidance, projects of the European Agricultural Guidance and Guarantee Fund, projects of the European Social Fund, projects of the European Regional Development Fund), except for transfers made in advance and impairment, shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2321</td> <td>Receivables for projects (measures) financed from foreign financial assistance and the European Union policy instruments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2328</td> <td>Impairment of receivables for projects (measures) financed from foreign financial assistance and the European Union policy instruments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2340</td> <td></td> <td>Receivables for taxes, fees, and other payments into budgets</td> <td>Receivables for taxes, fees, and other payments into budgets administered by the budget institutions and impairment, and receivables for the personal income tax to be distributed shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2341</td> <td>Receivables for immovable property tax</td> <td></td> </tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2360</td> <td></td> <td>Accrued revenues</td> <td>Revenues which have been earned until the end of the reporting period but for which the corroborative payment document has not been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2361</td> <td>Accrued interest revenues from deposits and balances on accounts</td> <td>Accrued interest revenues from balances on accounts, deposits upon demand, and deposits which have been deposited for a specific term and the initial maturity of which does not exceed 90 days shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2364</td> <td>Accrued revenues from services</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2368</td> <td>Accrued revenues from taxes, fees, and other payments into budgets</td> <td>Accrued revenues from the taxes, fees, and other payments into budgets administered by the budget institution shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2369</td> <td>Other accrued revenues</td> <td>Accrued revenues from finance lease, payments which have been deferred for more than 12 months, and other accrued revenues shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2370</td> <td></td> <td>Overpaid taxes, fees, and other payments into budgets</td> <td>Overpaid and prepaid taxes and their impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts separately according to the types of taxes</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2371</td> <td>Personal income tax</td> <td></td> </tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2379</td> <td>Other taxes, fees, and payments into budgets</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2380</td> <td></td> <td>Receivables from the personnel</td> <td>Settlements with employees of the budget institution for the issued prepayments, shortages and other receivables from the personnel and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2381</td> <td>Prepayments issued to employees</td> <td>Receivables for the issued prepayments (for example, for operating expenditures, travel expenditures, measures of operational activities, expenditures for ensuring the measures of operational activities) shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2388</td> <td>Impairment of receivables from the personnel</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2389</td> <td>Other receivables from the personnel</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2390</td> <td></td> <td>Other receivables</td> <td>Other previously non-classified receivables and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2391</td> <td>Receivables for overpayments to the recipients of social services</td> <td>This account shall be used only the State Social Insurance Agency</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2392</td> <td>Receivables for the remainder of pension and benefit settlements in disbursing institutions</td> <td>The benefits and pensions disbursed in advance shall be accounted in the account. This account shall be used only the State Social Insurance Agency</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2398</td> <td>Impairment of other receivables</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2399</td> <td>Other receivables</td> <td>Other previously non-classified receivables (for example, receivables for payments of the fund for the equalisation of local government finances, receivables from the security deposits paid to former employees) shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>2400</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Prepaid expenditures and prepayments for services and projects</td> <td>Payments made which are attributable to subsequent periods and prepayments paid for services and projects, including subsidies and grants for merchants, associations and foundations, shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2410</td> <td></td> <td>Prepaid expenditures for projects and measures financed from foreign financial assistance and the European Union policy instruments</td> <td>Receivables in relation to the fulfilment of the contractual conditions in the subsequent reporting periods, if the services for which payments had been made previously are received in parts or over the course of several months, and prepayments for measures financed from foreign financial assistance and the European Union policy instruments, except for transfers made in advance, shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2411</td> <td>Prepaid expenditures for projects of the Cohesion Fund</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2417</td> <td>Impairment of prepaid expenditures for projects and measures financed from foreign financial assistance and the European Union policy instruments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2419</td> <td>Other prepaid expenditures for projects and measures financed from foreign financial assistance and the European Union policy instruments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2420</td> <td></td> <td>Prepayments for services and other prepaid expenses</td> <td>Payments made which are attributable to subsequent periods and prepayments (advance payments) made for services (except for prepaid expenses and prepayments for measures financed from foreign financial assistance and the European Union policy instruments) and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2421</td> <td>Prepayments for services</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2422</td> <td>Prepaid expenditures for work remuneration settlements</td> <td>Work remuneration for employees of the budget institution for subsequent periods calculated and paid in accordance with the procedures laid down in laws and regulations shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2423</td> <td>Prepaid expenditures for social settlements</td> <td>Social insurance contributions for work remuneration calculated and paid, in accordance with the procedures laid down in laws and regulations, to employees of the budget institution, paid pensions, benefits, and other payments for social purposes for subsequent periods shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2427</td> <td>Impairment of prepayments for services</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2429</td> <td>Other prepaid expenditures</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2430</td> <td></td> <td>Transfers made in advance</td> <td>Transfers made in advance within the scope of the general government sector units shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2431</td> <td>Transfers for projects (measures) financed from the European Union policy instruments and other foreign financial assistance</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2432</td> <td>Other transfers</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2437</td> <td>Impairment of transfers made in advance</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>2500</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Short-term financial investments</td> <td>Financial investments which can be sold at any time and which are intended to be held for not more than one year from the balance sheet date, and also deposits which have been deposited for a specific term and the initial maturity of which exceeds 90 days shall be accounted in the group of accounts. Long-term financial investments expected to be disposed (sold) shall also be accounted in this group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2510</td> <td></td> <td>Participation in the capital of related capital companies</td> <td>Investments in capital companies in which the budget institution owns more than 50.001 per cent of the voting rights (subsidiary capital companies), if the State or local government has intended to hold them for not more than one year from the balance sheet date, and impairment of such investments shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2511</td> <td>Participation in the capital of related capital companies</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2519</td> <td>Impairment of participation in the capital of related capital companies</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2520</td> <td></td> <td>Participation in the capital of associated capital companies</td> <td>Investments in capital companies in which the budget institution owns from 20.001 to 50 per cent of the voting rights and which the State or local government has intended to hold for not more than one year from the balance sheet date and impairment of such investments shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2521</td> <td>Participation in the capital of associated capital companies</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2529</td> <td>Impairment of participation in the capital of associated capital companies</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2530</td> <td></td> <td>Current loans and the current portion of non-current loans</td> <td>The issued current loans and the portion of non-current loans the term for the repayment of which does not exceed one year from the balance sheet date, receivables, the accrued revenues for loan interest, liability, and servicing payments, and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2531</td> <td>Current loans to related capital companies</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2532</td> <td>Current loans to associated capital companies</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2534</td> <td>Impairment of current loans</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2535</td> <td>Receivables for interest and other payments for loans</td> <td>Current receivables for interest and other payments for loans which have been earned until the end of the reporting period and for which the corroborative payment document has been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2536</td> <td>Short-term accrued revenues from loans</td> <td>Accrued revenues from loan interest, liability, and servicing payments which have been earned until the end of the reporting period and for which the corroborative payment document has not been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2539</td> <td>Other current loans</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2540</td> <td></td> <td>Short-term financial investments in securities</td> <td>Investments in short-term securities and the portion of long-term securities the maturity or repayment term of which does not exceed one year from the balance sheet date, receivables, and accrued revenues for interest and other payments and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2545</td> <td>Receivables for interest and other payments for financial investments in securities</td> <td>Current receivables for interest and other payments for securities which have been earned until the end of the reporting period and for which the corroborative payment document has been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2546</td> <td>Accrued revenues from interest and other payments for financial investments in securities</td> <td>Accrued revenues from security interest and other payments which have been earned until the end of the reporting period and for which the corroborative payment document has not been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2548</td> <td>Impairment of short-term financial investments in securities</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2549</td> <td>Other short-term financial investments in securities</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2550</td> <td></td> <td>Other short-term financial investments</td> <td>Short-term financial investments in international financial institutions, other short-term financial investments, and the portion of long-term financial investments the expected holding limit of which does not exceed one year from the balance sheet date, receivables, and accrued revenues from interest and other payments and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2551</td> <td>Short-term financial investments in international financial institutions</td> <td>Investments in international financial institutions (the World Bank (WB), the Council of Europe Development Bank (CEDB), the Multilateral Investment Guarantee Agency (MIGA), the European Bank for Reconstruction and Development (EBRD), and others, except for the International Monetary Fund (IMF)), if they are expected to be held for not more than one year from the balance sheet date, shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2554</td> <td>Current receivables for tier 2 funded pensions</td> <td>This account shall be used only the State Social Insurance Agency</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2555</td> <td>Receivables for interest and other payments for other financial investments</td> <td>Receivables for interest and other payments for other financial investments which have been earned until the end of the reporting period and for which the corroborative payment document has been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2556</td> <td>Accrued revenues from other short-term financial investments</td> <td>Accrued revenues from interest and other payments of other financial investments which have been earned until the end of the reporting period and for which the corroborative payment document has not been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2558</td> <td>Impairment of other short-term financial investments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2559</td> <td>Other short-term financial investments</td> <td>Investments in the capital of capital companies in which the budget institution owns less than 20 per cent of the voting rights, if they are intended to be held for not longer than one year from the balance sheet date, State investments in the International Monetary Fund (IMF), derived financial instruments, and other previously non-classified short-term financial investments and the portion of long-term financial investments the holding or maturity limit of which does not exceed one year from the balance sheet date shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2560</td> <td></td> <td>Short-term deposits</td> <td>Short-term deposits which have been deposited for a specific term and the initial maturity of which does not exceed 90 days, and also current portion of non-current deposits shall be accounted in the group of accounts. If the residual maturity of the deposit is less than 90 days, its accounting in this account shall be continued</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2565</td> <td>Receivables for interest and other payments for deposits</td> <td>Current receivables for interest and other payments for deposits which have been earned until the end of the reporting period and for which the corroborative payment document has been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2566</td> <td>Accrued revenues from interest and other payments for deposits</td> <td>Accrued revenues from deposit interest and other payments which have been earned until the end of the reporting period and for which the corroborative payment document has not been prepared until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2568</td> <td>Impairment of short-term deposits</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2569</td> <td>Short-term deposits</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2570</td> <td></td> <td>Prepayments for short-term financial investments</td> <td>Prepayments for short-term financial investments and impairment shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2571</td> <td>Prepayments for short-term financial investments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2577</td> <td>Impairment of prepayments for short-term financial investments</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2590</td> <td></td> <td>Current receivables for the derived financial instruments</td> <td>Current receivables for the derived financial instruments shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>2600</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Cash</td> <td>Cash on hand and current accounts, deposits in financial institutions in breakdown by the types of budgets, and also such cash which is not the property of the budget institution, but is in the cash on hand or accounts of the budget institution for a definite period (for example, the tender/security deposits, election deposits, pensions of persons in nursing homes, funds seized from detained persons until a court decision, cash received from debtors for previous reporting periods which are to be paid into the State budget revenues, and also rewards received from State officials for the redemption of gifts in accordance with the procedures laid down in legal acts, funds allocated only to State budget institutions under the Cabinet orders from the State privatisation fund, budget funds received from other budget institutions in accordance with the appropriation procedures under a financing plan, deposited cash that is in the possession of a customer) shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2610</td> <td></td> <td>Cash on hand</td> <td>Cash on hand in breakdown by budget types shall be accounted in the accounts of the group of accounts and cash which does not belong thereto shall also be accounted if they are on hand of the budget institution</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2611</td> <td>Funds of the basic budget</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2612</td> <td>Funds of the special budget</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2613</td> <td>Funds of donations and gifts</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2614</td> <td>Funds of other budgets</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2620</td> <td></td> <td>Current accounts in the Treasury, credit institutions, or other institutions</td> <td>Cash in current accounts and deposits upon demand (including accounts to which payment cards have been linked) in breakdown by the budget types and cash received by the budget institution which does not belong thereto shall be accounted in the group of accounts if such cash is in the current accounts of the budget institution</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2621</td> <td>Funds of the basic budget</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2622</td> <td>Funds of the special budget</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2623</td> <td>Funds of donations and gifts</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2624</td> <td>Funds of other budgets</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2630</td> <td></td> <td>Cash equivalents</td> <td>Deposits (also with the Treasury and credit institutions) which have been deposited for a specific term and the initial maturity of which does not exceed 90 days shall be accounted in the group of accounts in breakdown by budget types, including the funds received by the budget institution which do not belong thereto if they have been placed in deposits</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2631</td> <td>Funds of the basic budget</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2632</td> <td>Funds of the special budget</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2633</td> <td>Funds of donations and gifts</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2634</td> <td>Funds of other budgets</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2670</td> <td></td> <td>Cash en route</td> <td>Cash en route in breakdown by the budget types shall be accounted in the group of accounts. Cash en route shall be accounted if the budget institution makes transfer before the end of the reporting period and a confirmation that the payment has been received in this reporting period has not been received. Cash en route shall be accounted if the contract provides that liabilities are to be considered extinguished only when the addressee has received the cash in his or her account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2671</td> <td>Funds of the basic budget</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2672</td> <td>Funds of the special budget</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2673</td> <td>Funds of donations and gifts</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>2674</td> <td>Funds of other budgets</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>2690</td> <td></td> <td>Appropriations to local government budget institutions</td> <td>Appropriations to local governments for the financing of subordinate institutions shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>3000</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Equity</td> <td>The equity capital (net assets) which is determined as the residual part of the assets after deduction of all liabilities shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>3300</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Reserves</td> <td>The revaluation reserve of long-term financial instruments and other reserves shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>3320</td> <td></td> <td>Fair value reserve of financial instruments</td> <td>Changes in the fair value of financial instruments in cases where they are accounted in the equity capital shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>3360</td> <td></td> <td>Other reserves</td> <td>Increase in the value arisen from the revaluation of long-term financial investments (under the equity method) shall be accounted in the account if there are changes in the equity capital of the capital company which do not affect the result of the reporting period of the capital company (for example, revaluation of property, plant and equipment)</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>3500</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Surplus or deficit</td> <td>Surplus or deficit of the reporting year and previous years shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>3510</td> <td></td> <td>Surplus or deficit of previous reporting years</td> <td>Surplus or deficit of previous years for the relevant type of budget shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>3511</td> <td colspan="2">[6 September 2022]</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>3512</td> <td colspan="2">[6 September 2022]</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>3513</td> <td colspan="2">[6 September 2022]</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>3514</td> <td colspan="2">[6 September 2022]</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>3520</td> <td></td> <td>Surplus or deficit of the reporting year</td> <td>Surplus or deficit of the reporting year for the relevant type of budget shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>3521</td> <td colspan="2">[6 September 2022]</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>3522</td> <td colspan="2">[6 September 2022]</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>3523</td> <td colspan="2">[6 September 2022]</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>3524</td> <td colspan="2">[6 September 2022]</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>4000</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Provisions</td> <td>Liabilities for which an accurate settlement period and amount is not known shall be accounted in the account. Such liabilities shall relate to transactions of the reporting year or previous years and other past events and are expected or known during the preparation of the statement, but an accurate value thereof or the date of the occurrence or settlement thereof are not known</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td>5000</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Liabilities</td> <td>Present liabilities which have arisen from past transactions (events) and for the settlement of which an outflow of resources is required shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>5100</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Non-current liabilities</td> <td>Liabilities which are expected to be settled more than one year after the balance sheet date shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>5110</td> <td></td> <td>Long-term borrowings</td> <td>Borrowings with the term for the repayment of which exceeds one year after the balance sheet date and liabilities and accrued expenditures from interest and other payments for borrowings shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>5114</td> <td>Previously guaranteed long-term borrowings</td> <td>The non-current portion (principal amount) of such borrowings for which a guarantee has been previously provided and the obligation to repay them has set in shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>5115</td> <td>Non-current liabilities for interest and other payments for borrowings</td> <td>Expenditures for interest and other payments which have arisen until the end of the reporting period but are due for settlement more than one year after the balance sheet date and for which the corroborative payment document has been issued until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>5116</td> <td>Non-current accrued liabilities for interest and other payments for borrowings</td> <td>Accrued expenditures from interest and other payments which have arisen until the end of the reporting period but are due for settlement more than one year after the balance sheet date and for which the corroborative payment document has not been issued until the end of the reporting period shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>5119</td> <td>Long-term borrowings</td> <td>The principal amount of borrowings with a repayment limit which exceeds one year after the balance sheet date shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>5130</td> <td></td> <td>Non-current liabilities towards suppliers and contractors</td> <td>Liabilities for the received goods and services the settlement term of which exceeds one year from the balance sheet date shall be accounted in the group of accounts</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>5131</td> <td>Non-current liabilities towards suppliers and contractors</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <td></td> <td></td> <td>8190</td> <td></td> <td>Other financial revenues</td> <td>Revenues from the surplus of financial investments established as a result of the taking of inventory, initial recognition of financial investments, write-down of the impairment of financial investments, extinguishing of borrowings, and other previously non-classified financial revenues, including revenues recognised in hedge accounting, shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td>8200</td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>Financial expenditures</td> <td></td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>8210</td> <td></td> <td>Expenditures from currency exchange rate fluctuations</td> <td>Expenditures arising from currency exchange rate fluctuations in settling accounts for monetary items (all amounts of assets and liabilities expressed in foreign currency units which are to be received or paid in foreign currency) or in recalculating the monetary items at the end of the reporting period according to the currency exchange rate which is different from the rate according to which the monetary item was initially recognised or recalculated at the end of the previous reporting period shall be accounted in the group of accounts. Such expenditures shall be classified according to the budget types</td> </tr>
 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td></td> <td>8211</td> <td>Expenditures from currency exchange rate fluctuations of the basic budget</td> <td></td> </tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>8290</td> <td></td> <td>Other financial expenditures</td> <td>Expenditures from the impairment of financial investments and other previously non-classified financial expenditures which arise from establishing shortages of financial investments during the taking of inventory, extinguishing loans, and other cases, including expenditures recognised in hedge accounting, shall be accounted in the account</td> </tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>8410</td> <td></td> <td>Revenues from the values received and transferred without reward</td> <td></td> </tr>

 <td></td> <td></td> <td>8510</td> <td></td> <td>Revenues from the sale of the non-financial assets intended for disposal</td> <td>The account shall be used in the accounting of revenues from the sale of intangible investments, property, plant and equipment, investment properties, biological assets, and inventories</td> </tr>
 <tr>

 <tr>
 <td></td> <td></td> <td>8550</td> <td></td> <td>Revenues from the write-down of the impairment of assets</td> <td>The group of accounts shall be used for the write-down of the impairment of non-financial assets and receivables, and prepayments</td> </tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>

 <tr>
